In pursuance of Rule 18 of the Rules made by the Government of India under Section 28 of the Public Debt Act, 1944 ard publish- 
ed in the Gazette of India of the 20th April 1946 [as amended under Notification No. F. (8) (70)-B/52 dated the 29th April 1954] the 
following list (for the quarter ended 30th June 1973) is hereby advertised of securities lost etc. in respect of which prima fade ground* 
exist for believing that the securities have been lost and that the claim of applicants is just. All persons other than the respective clai- 
mants named below who have any claim upon these securities should communicate imnicdiatley with the Chief Accountant, Reserve Bank 
of India, Central Office, Department of Accounts and Expenditure, Central Debt Section, Bombay. 


The list has been divided into two parts — part ‘A’ being securities now advertised for the first time and part ‘B’ the list of securities 
previously advertised.

STATE BANK OF INDIA 
Central Office 

Bombay, the 29th May 1975 
NOTICE 

In terms of Section 32 of the State Bank of India 
(Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959, the State Bank of India 
has appointed Shri M, D, Dalai, General Manager 
(Planning), State Bank of India, New Delhi Local 
Head Office, to officiate as Managing Director of Stale 
Bank of Patiala with effect from the 27th May, 1975 
to 26th July, 1975 (inclusive) vice Shri S. D. Ganda 
granted leave. 

Sd/- ILLEGIBLE 
Managing Director 


Bombay, the 21st April 1975 

SBD/No. 7/1975.— In pursuance of sub-section (2) 
of Section 26 of the State Bank of India (Subsidiary 
Banks) Act, 1959 (38 of 1959), the term of appoint- 
ment of Shri V. P. Amin, ‘Nima’, Sardar Patel Colony, 
Near Stadium, Ahmedabad-1 4, nominated as a Direc- 
tor on the Board of the State Bank of Saurashtra 
under clause (c) of sub-section (1) of Section 25 of 
the Act (ibid) expires on the 19th June 1975. 


2. It is hereby notified for general information that, 
in pursuance of clause (c) of sub-section (1) of Sec- 
tion 25 of the Act (ibid), the State Bank of India, in 
consultation with the Reserve Bank of India, has re- 
nominated Shri V. P, Amin as a Director on the Board 
of the State Bank of Saurashtra for a further term of 
three years from the 20th June 1975 to 19th June 1973 
(inclusive). 

Sd/- ILLEGIBLE 
Managing Director 


Bombay, the 17th May 1975 

SBD. No. 8/1975, — It is hereby notified for general 
information that under proviso to clause (d) of sub- 
section (1) of Section 25 of the State Bank of India 
(Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959 (38 of 1959), the State 
Bank of India, in consultation with the Reserve Bank 
of India, hereby nominates Shri M. F. Tamboli, C/o. 
Steelcast Bhavnagar (Pvt.) Ltd., Bhavnagar, as a 
Director of the State Bank of Saurashtra for a term of 
three years from the 17th May 1975 to the 16th May 
1978 (inclusive) .in place of Shri R. B. Shah, who will 
cease to be a Director from today. 

R. K. TALWAR 
Chairman

publication in the Official Gazette.
